EG algorithm details:
```
def eg_search(a0, budget, t_start, betas=(20,50,100,200,500,1000,2000), step_init=0.5, maxiter=None, verbose=1000):
   a = a0.copy(); n=len(a); S=a.sum(); a/=S
   best = score_only(a); best_a=a.copy()
   # v = log(a) maybe with floor
   v = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
   # maybe center v for stability: v -= np.log(v.max())? Actually a normalized; v=log a can be ~ -9. For zeros floor.
   for beta in betas:
      # continue while time
      # maybe reset step based on gradient norm
      step = step_init  # maybe scale
      for it in range(...):
         if time > deadline: break
         A = rfft(a, nfft1)
         c = irfft(A*A, nfft1)[:2n-1]
         M = c.max()
         score = 2*n*M
         if score < best: update
         # p
         z = beta*(c-M)
         z -= z.max()
         e = np.exp(z)
         p = e/e.sum()  # if beta low maybe broad; if high top
         # E=dot(p,c)
         E = float(p @ c)
         # r via FFT with p_rev
         # nfft_r = 1 << (3*n-2 -1).bit_length()
         Pr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], nfft_r)
         Ar = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft_r)
         conv = np.fft.irfft(Ar*Pr, nfft_r)
         r = conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
         # grad_a = 4*n*(r - E*a)  (since S=1)
         g = r - E*a
         # normalize gradient
         gn = np.linalg.norm(g, ord=1)
         if gn==0: break
         g = g / gn  # scale to O(1)
         # update v: v -= step * g? Need step scale. Since g normalized, step maybe 0.1.
         # But g entries can be large? normalized mean. Use step = 0.5 * initial? We'll line search.
         # prop steps [step, step/2, step/4]
         # To avoid large, step = min(step, 1.0) maybe
         candidates...
      step *=0.7? or adapt
```

Let's implement core helpers precisely.

`fast_conv(a)`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    m = 2*n - 1
    nfft = 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, nfft)
    return c[:m]
```

`score_conv(a,c)`:
```
def score_conv(a,c):
    n = len(a); S=a.sum(); M=float(c.max())
    if S < 1e-12: return np.inf
    return 2.0*n*M/(S*S)
```
For internal normalized sum1, `2nM`.

`normalize_seq(a)`:
```
def normalize_seq(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: a.fill(1.0/len(a))
    else: a = a/S
    return a
```
But if a has extremely small values, okay.

`init_v_from_a(a)`:
```
def init_v_from_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,None); a=a/sum
    v=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
    return v - v.mean()
```

`softmax_from_v(v)`:
```
def softmax_from_v(v):
    v = np.asarray(v,float)
    v = v - v.mean()  # remove scale? But softmax invariant to mean; center for stability
    v = v - v.max()
    e = np.exp(v)
    S=e.sum()
    if S<=0: return np.ones(len(v))/len(v)
    return e/S
```
The centering changes actual a? softmax invariant to adding constant, not subtracting mean? Subtracting mean is same as adding constant -mean, invariant. Good. It can change huge values by making mean zero, stable.

`compute_r(a,p)`:
```
def compute_r(a,p):
    n=len(a); L=len(p)  # should 2n-1
    req = n + L - 1  # 3n-2
    nfft = 1 << (req-1).bit_length()
    pr = p[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    P = np.fft.rfft(pr, nfft)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A*P, nfft)
    # output indices L-1-i = (2n-2)-i for i=0..n-1 -> slice [n-1:2n-1] reversed? L-1=2n-2. slice start n-1 end 2n-1 contains indices n-1..2n-2. reversed -> 2n-2..n-1.
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Let's test n=2 earlier: L=3, slice conv[1:3] reversed = indices 2,1 => r0,r1. good.

`lse_grad_v(v,beta)`:
```
def lse_grad_v(v, beta):
    a = softmax_from_v(v)
    n = len(a)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    z = beta*(c-cmax)
    z = z - z.max()
    e = np.exp(z)
    p = e/e.sum()
    L = float(cmax + np.log(e.sum())/beta)  # since z shifted? Need careful: if zshifted = beta*(c-cmax)-max(beta*(c-cmax)), e=exp(zshifted), logsum = maxshift + log(e.sum()). L = cmax + (maxshift + log(e.sum()))/beta. If we set z = beta*(c-cmax); zmax = z.max(); e=exp(z-zmax); L=cmax + (zmax + log(e.sum()))/beta.
```

Let's think of a more mathematically targeted improvement: **active set LP via SOTA**. We can implement a simple best-response using **multiplicative weights** to update a, which may outperform L-BFGS. Let's derive an algorithm from SOTA that is efficient:
For current a, M=max c. Solve dual covering `min 1^T y s.t. h(y)_i = (a * y)_i >=1, y>=0`. We can find y using **proximal subgradient** with entropy:
   Initialize y = uniform over active lags.
   For its: h = compute_r(y? wait h_i = ∑_k a_{k-i} y_k. This is same form r but with p replaced by y. We can compute via FFT. We need minimize sum y with h_i>=1. This is like finding a nonnegative sequence y such that its convolution with a is at least 1. The objective sum y. If a sum1, constant y=1 works (sum=2n-1) because h_i ~∑a=1. We can reduce sum y by putting mass where it covers many i. This is a covering problem. We can solve with subgradient: minimize f(y)=sum y + ρ∑max(0,1-h_i). Subgradient: 1 - ρ [h_i<1 indicator]*a_{k-i}. Also y>=0. Use multiplicative updates to keep y nonnegative: log y_k <- log y_k - η (1 - ρ∑_{i:h_i<1} a_{k-i}). This is like expgradient. Might find y with smaller sum.
Then y normalized to p = y/sum y. The best response g support where h_i≈1; set g_i = 1 if h_i close to1 else 0. Then rescale g to sum1 and mix with a. This exactly resembles SOTA. Could be more global.

Alternatively, instead of solving full LP, use **alternating p and a** where p is y normalized. The KKT condition for a at optimum with M: p (dual for max c?) and a satisfy H_p a = constant. We can update a by solving/approxiating H_p a constant. And update p by solving covering for current a: H_a p? Actually H_p a_i = ∑_k p_k a_{k-i}. For p to be optimal dual for a, maybe H_a p_k? Let's see. The maximum M(a)=max c_k. The dual of min M subject? The subgradient p of M at a must satisfy p_k>0 => c_k=M. For a to be optimal under p, H_p a constant. This is a saddle point: minimize over a E_p(a)/S^2, maximize over p∈Δ E_p? Actually M(a)=max_k c_k = max_{p∈Δ} E_p(a)/S^2 (with S=1). We need min_a max_p E_p(a). This is a convex-concave game? E_p(a) is quadratic not jointly convex. But we can play best responses:
- Given a, choose p to maximize E_p(a). That's just p on max lags, not covering. To improve a, p should be a subgradient. But for descent, any p at active max. To escape, maybe choose p from dual of best-response? The SOTA uses p from LP, not simply max.
- Given p, best a minimizes E_p(a)/S^2 under a≥0. This is a quadratic program. We can approximate by multiplicative updates.
This alternation may be better. We can implement p as softmax over c with β; a by many multiplicative exponentials of gradient. That's our EG.

Could solve `min_a E_p(a)` with **semidefinite?** Not.

`fast_conv(a)`, `score_from_normalized(a)` etc.

`softmax_from_v(v)`:
```
def softmax_from_v(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    v = v - np.mean(v)
    vmax = np.max(v)
    e = np.exp(v - vmax)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.ones(n)/n
    a = e / s
    return a
```

`init_v_from_a(a)`:
```
def init_v_from_a(a, floor=1e-10):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size ==0: return np.array([])
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    S = a.sum()
    if S < 1e-12:
       a = np.ones_like(a)
    else: a=a/S
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = a/a.sum()
    v = np.log(a)
    return v - np.mean(v)
```
Floor may slightly alter best. For final, use a not floor. OK.

`fast_conv` as above. If n small, maybe use direct for n<128 to avoid FFT overhead? Could:
```
if n <= 128: return np.convolve(a,a)
```
For small, faster.

`compute_r(a,p)`: If p is sparse top K, could direct. But general. Ensure p length 2n-1; if smaller pad.
```
def compute_r(a,p):
   n=len(a)
   p = np.asarray(p,float)
   if p.size < 2*n-1: pad
   req = n + len(p) -1
   nfft = 1 << (req-1).bit_length()
   pr = p[::-1]
   A = np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
   P = np.fft.rfft(pr,nfft)
   conv = np.fft.irfft(A*P,nfft)
   # if p length L maybe L not 2n-1; index L-1-i.
   return conv[len(p)-1:len(p)-1+n:-1]? Wait if L=2n-1, slice [L-1 - (n-1) : L] = [n-1:2n-1]. Reversed.
```
We can do:
```
start = len(p) - n  # L-n = (2n-1)-n = n-1
end = len(p)      # 2n-1
r = conv[start:end][::-1]
```
If L=2n-1, start=n-1, end=2n-1. Good. If other, works.

`lse_grad_v`:
```
def lse_grad_v(v,beta):
   a=softmax_from_v(v)
   n=len(a)
   if n==0: return np.inf, np.array([])
   c=fast_conv(a)
   cmax=float(c.max())
   z=beta*(c-cmax)
   zmax=float(z.max())
   z=z-zmax
   e=np.exp(z)
   esum=float(e.sum())
   if esum<=0: p=np.full_like(c,1/len(c)); L=cmax
   else: p=e/esum; L=cmax+(zmax+np.log(esum))/beta
   r=compute_r(a,p)
   grad_a=4.0*n*(r - L*a)
   # project to tangent of simplex for softmax: grad_v = a*(grad_a - dot(a,grad_a))
   dot=float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
   grad_v=a*(grad_a-dot)
   grad_v -= np.mean(grad_v)
   scale=float(n)
   score=2.0*n*L*scale
   return score, grad_v
```
Check: For beta small, p uniform, E=sum c/(2n-1)=1/(2n-1). Score=2n/(2n-1) ~1. But actual score max maybe 2. Smooth objective lower, okay for continuation. But gradient of smooth p-weighted objective, not exact. Good.

Potential bug: `scale=n` makes objective ~2n^2 L; for n=7000, uniform L=1/n => score ~14000. Fine.

`adam_v_search`:
```
def adam_v_search(v0, beta, n_steps, lr, deadline, verbose=1000, eps=1e-8):
    if not HAVE? no need.
    v = np.asarray(v0,float).copy()
    v = v - v.mean()
    n=len(v)
    m=np.zeros(n); u=np.zeros(n)
    best_v=v.copy()
    best_a=softmax_from_v(v)
    best_sc=exact_score(best_a)
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999
    for t in range(1,n_steps+1):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       f,g = lse_grad_v(v,beta)
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*g
       u=b2*u+(1-b2)*g*g
       mhat=m/(1-b1**t)
       uhat=u/(1-b2**t)
       step=lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(uhat)+eps)
       v = v - step
       v = v - v.mean()
       # optional clip to [-50,50]
       if t == 10 or np.max(np.abs(v)) > 40: v=np.clip(v,-40,40); v-=v.mean()
       if t % verbose ==0:
          a=softmax_from_v(v); sc=exact_score(a)
          if sc<best_sc: best...; print
    final check
    return best_sc,best_v,best_a
```
But if `g` has zeros for coordinates, Adam won't move zeros; okay.

`run_lbfgs_v`:
```
def run_lbfgs_v(v0,beta,maxiter=1000,maxfun=5000,prefix=''):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return ...
    v0=np.asarray(v0,float).copy(); v0-=v0.mean()
    a=softmax_from_v(v0); sc=exact_score(a)
    bounds=None
    options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-5,'maxls':50}
    res=minimize(fun_v_lse, v0, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
    v1=np.asarray(res.x,float); v1-=v1.mean()
    a=softmax_from_v(v1); sc=exact_score(a)
    return sc,v1,a,res
```
Need top-level `fun_v_lse(v,beta)` returns tuple. Ensure no exception; if returns inf, maybe.

`fun_v_lse`:
```
def fun_v_lse(v,beta):
    try:
       f,g=lse_grad_v(v,beta)
       if not np.isfinite(f):
           return 1e20, np.zeros_like(v)
       return f,g
    except Exception:
       return 1e20, np.zeros_like(v)
```
But `np.zeros_like(v)` okay.

`make_shape(n, kind, rng=None)`:
```
def make_shape(n, kind=0, rng=None):
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   if n==1: return np.array([1.0])
   if kind==0: a=np.ones(n)
   elif kind==1: a=1.0+0.9*np.cos(np.pi*x/(n-1)) # high edges low center
   elif kind==2: a=1.0+0.8*np.cos(2*np.pi*x/(n-1))
   elif kind==3: a=np.abs(np.sin(np.pi*x/(n-1))) # high center
   elif kind==4:
       if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
       a=1.0+0.5*rng.standard_normal(n)
   elif kind==5:
       # two Gaussians near edges
       c1=n*0.2; c2=n*0.8; w=n*0.12
       a=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c1)/w)**2)+np.exp(-0.5*((x-c2)/w)**2)
   elif kind==6:
       # four waves random
       a=1+0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*np.arange(n)*3/n)+...
   a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<1e-12: a=np.ones(n)
   else: a/=S
   return a
```

def exact_subgrad_top(a, top_k=10):
   n=len(a); c=fast_conv(a); M=c.max(); sorted_idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
   weights = c[sorted_idx] - M + 1.0; weights/=weights.sum()
   p=np.zeros(2n-1); for k,w in zip: p[k]=w
   r=compute_r(a,p)
   E=dot(p,c)
   grad=4*n*(r-E*a)
   return M, grad, p
```

Coordinate descent exact score: Need not use LSE. Implement:
```
def coordinate_descent(a_in, deadline, top_k=20, max_sweeps=50, verbose=10):
   a=a_in.copy(); n=len(a); best=exact_score(a)
   for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
      if time>deadline: break
      c=fast_conv(a); M=c.max(); sorted_idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
      weights=c[sorted_idx]-M+1.0; weights/=weights.sum()
      p=np.zeros(2*n-1); for k,w zip: p[k]=w
      r=compute_r(a,p); E=float(np.dot(p,c)); grad=4*n*a*(r-E*a)
      order=np.argsort(np.abs(grad))[::-1][:top_k]
      for j in order:
          if time>deadline: break
          old=a[j]
          ratios=[0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85,1.0,1.15,1.3,1.6,2.0]
          cand_vals=old*ratios
          # also maybe 0
          best_val=old; best_sc=best
          for val in cand_vals:
              if val<0 or not finite: continue
              a[j]=val; s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: continue; a2=a/s; sc=exact_score(a2)
              if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_val=val; a2best=a2.copy()
          a=a2best if improved else normalize(a)
          if best_sc<best: best=best_sc; if best_sc<best_best? update? We can return best_a after loop.
      # after sweep normalize
   return best_sc,a
```
This is O(sweeps * top_k * candidates * FFT). Too heavy. Limit top_k=10, candidates=5. Deadline maybe 120s. Use n maybe 7000. Could be okay.

But coordinate descent may degrade due dealing with sum. We normalize after each coordinate; but when updating `a[j]`, other values unchanged, then normalized. When trying candidates, we need copy to not interfere. We'll do:
```
base=a.copy()
for val in ratios*base[j]:
   a_try=base.copy(); a_try[j]=val; a_try/=a_try.sum(); sc=...
   if best: best_try=a_try.copy()
a=best_try if better
```
This copies n per candidate (7000*5*10=350k floats per sweep) okay.

Random block search top-level:
```
def block_search(a_in, deadline, n_trials=2000, rng=None):
   best=a; sc
   n=len(a); for t:
     a_try=best.copy()
     # random block multiply
     width=np.exp2(rng.integers(2, 12)) * max(1,n//2048) maybe
     width=min(width,n//2)
     center=int(rng.integers(width,n-width))
     a_try[center:center+width] *= rng.uniform(0.3,2.0)
     normalize; sc=exact_score
     if sc<best_sc*(1-1e-8): best=...; sc=...
   return best

def run_lbfgs_v(v0,beta,maxiter=1000,maxfun=5000,gscale=1.0):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return exact_score(softmax(v0)), v0, softmax(v0)
    v0=np.asarray(v0,float).copy(); v0-=np.mean(v0)
    a=softmax_from_v(v0); sc0=exact_score(a)
    options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'maxfun': int(maxfun), 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-6, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False}
    try:
       res=minimize(fun_v_lse, v0, args=(beta, gscale), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
       v1=np.asarray(res.x,float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(v1)): v1=v0
       v1-=np.mean(v1)
    except Exception:
       v1=v0
    a1=softmax_from_v(v1); sc1=exact_score(a1)
    if not np.isfinite(sc1) or sc1>=sc0:
       return sc0,v0,a
    return sc1,v1,a1
```

```
def resample_sequence(a,new_n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   old=len(a)
   if old==new_n: return a.copy()
   if old==1: return np.full(new_n,float(a[0])/new_n)? sum? We want interpolation of shape; scale invariant. Simpler: if old==1 return np.ones(new_n)/new_n.
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
   a_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   a_new=np.clip(a_new,0,1000)
   S=a_new.sum(); if S<1e-12: uniform; else a_new/S
   return a_new
```
Interpolation preserves sum approx.

`coordinate_descent` implementation details:
```
def coordinate_descent(a_in, deadline, top_k=15, max_sweeps=100, ratios=None):
   a=np.asarray(a_in,float).copy(); n=len(a); best_sc=exact_score(a); best_a=a.copy()
   if ratios is None: ratios=np.array([0.0,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85,1.15,1.4,1.8])
   for sweep...
      c=fast_conv(a); M=c.max(); idx=...
      p...
      r=compute_r(a,p); E=...
      grad=4*n*a*(r-E*a)
      order=...
      for j in order:
         old=a[j]
         vals=old*ratios
         if old < 10.0/n: vals=np.concatenate((vals,[0.01/n,0.05/n,0.2/n,1.0/n]))
         best_val=old; best_sc_j=best_sc
         for val in vals:
             if val<0 or not finite: continue
             if val==old: continue
             a[j]=val
             sc=exact_score(a)  # uses S
             if sc<best_sc_j: best_sc_j=sc; best_val=val; best_a_j=a.copy()
         if best_sc_j < best_sc - 1e-15:
             a=best_a_j.copy(); best_sc=best_sc_j
         else:
             a[j]=old
      a_norm=a/a.sum(); if better? Actually score invariant, no need normalize for best. But keep normalized by best_a/=sum for next gradient? gradient formulas assume normalized? We derived for S=1. We should normalize a at end of sweep: a = a/a.sum(); best_sc = exact_score(a) (same). best_a=best_a/sum.
   return best_sc,best_a
```
When computing grad we should use normalized a. We'll normalize at start of sweep. Good.

`block_search`:
```
def block_search(a_in, deadline, rng, max_trials=50000):
   a=normalize(a_in); best=exact_score(a); best_a=a.copy(); n=len(a)
   for t in range(max_trials):
      if time>deadline: break
      a_try=best_a.copy()
      # width random log scale
      logw = np.log2(max(4,n//64)) + rng.uniform(0, np.log2(n//4))
      w=int(max(4, min(n//2, 2**logw)))
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
      a_try[start:start+w] *= rng.uniform(0.2,3.0)
      # maybe global scale unchanged after normalize
      S=a_try.sum(); if S<1e-12: continue
      a_try/=S
      sc=exact_score(a_try)
      if sc < best - 1e-12:
         best=sc; best_a=a_try.copy()
         print occasionally
   return best,best_a
```
This uses exact score each trial. 5000 trials * FFT maybe 5000*0.2ms=1s if fast; but Python exact_score conv overhead maybe 0.5ms =>2.5s. 50000 maybe 25s. Good.

def finalize_best(candidates, t_start, budget):
   best_val=np.inf; best_list=None
   for cand in candidates:
      if cand is None or len(cand)==0: continue
      lst=[float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in np.asarray(cand).ravel()]

          best_val=val; best_list=lst

But to be safe, we can implement top-level `fun_v_lse_obj` returning (f,g) and `jac_v_lse` returning gradient, and call with `jac=jac_v_lse`. The function can call lse_grad_v and return f; jac call it again returning g. This doubles evaluations? No, scipy can use separate jac, each iteration calls fun and jac separately (2 gradient computations). But with tuple, one call. To be safe and efficient, use tuple; previous code used it. OK.

But if `minimize` with `args` and `jac=True` expects fun returns only f and jac callable? Actually if `jac` is callable, it is called. If `jac=True`, it uses gradient returned by fun if available. I think tuple works. We can also set `jac=False`? no.

To support both, we can make `fun_v_lse` return tuple only. If scipy complains, we can't know. Previous code used this pattern, likely works.

`exact_score`:
```
def exact_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fast_conv(a)
    M=float(c.max())
    return float(2.0*len(a)*M/(S*S))
```
If a huge length with zeros, fast_conv maybe. If `M` negative due roundoff, set max(0,M).

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if n <= 128:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        m=2*n-1
        nfft=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
        A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
        c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,nfft)[:m]
    return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
For small, `np.convolve` of floats okay.

`compute_r` for p length L. If n small direct? We can use direct for n<=128 maybe? But p loops. Use FFT generic. Need if `p.size==0`. Use `L=len(p)`.
```
def compute_r(a,p):
    n=len(a); L=len(p)
    req=n+L-1
    nfft=1 << (req-1).bit_length()
    pr=p[::-1]
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    P=np.fft.rfft(pr,nfft)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,nfft)
    start=L-n
    end=L
    r=conv[start:end][::-1]
    return np.maximum(r,0.0)? r should nonnegative if p and a nonnegative. But p can have tiny negative? p from exp positive. Use max(0).
```
If r small negative due FFT, max 0.

`lse_grad_v`:
```
def lse_grad_v(v,beta,gscale=1.0):
   a=softmax_from_v(v)
   n=len(a)
   if n==0: return np.inf, np.array([])
   c=fast_conv(a)
   cmax=float(c.max())
   if beta <= 0:
       # use exact subgradient? set p at max
       p=np.zeros_like(c); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; L=cmax
   else:
       z=beta*(c-cmax)
       zmax=float(np.max(z))
       z-=zmax
       e=np.exp(z)
       esum=float(e.sum())
       if esum <= 0 or not np.isfinite(esum):
           p=np.ones_like(c)/len(c); L=float(np.mean(c))
       else:
           p=e/esum
           L=float(cmax + (zmax + float(np.log(esum)))/beta)
   r=compute_r(a,p)
   grad=4.0*n*a*(r - L*a)
   grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
       grad=np.zeros(n)
   if gscale != 1.0:
       grad=grad*gscale
       f=float(2.0*n*L*gscale)
   else: f=float(2.0*n*L)
   return f, grad
```
If `L` can be slightly > cmax? LSE smooth max >=cmax? Yes. For beta finite, L=cmax + positive. Good.

`softmax_from_v`: If v contains NaN, return uniform.
```
def softmax_from_v(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): v=np.zeros(n)
    v=v-np.mean(v)
    mx=np.max(v)
    e=np.exp(v-mx)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(n)/n
    return e/s
```

`init_v_from_a`:
```
def init_v_from_a(a,floor=1e-10):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.array([])
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000); S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: a=np.ones_like(a)
    else: a=a/S
    a=np.maximum(a,floor); a=a/a.sum()
    v=np.log(a)
    return v-np.mean(v)
```
Floor 1e-10 might be too large for n=20000 (mass 1e-10 each sum 2e-6, negligible). Good.

`fun_v_lse`:
```
def fun_v_lse(v,beta,gscale=1.0):
    try:
       f,g=lse_grad_v(v,beta,gscale)
       if not np.isfinite(f):
          f=1e30; g=np.zeros_like(v)
       return f,g
    except Exception:
       return 1e30, np.zeros_like(v)
```

`adam_v_search`:
Need exact score for best; use `exact_score(a)`.
```
def adam_v_search(v0,beta,n_steps,lr,deadline,verbose=5000):
    v=np.asarray(v0,float).copy()
    if v.size==0: return np.inf,v,np.array([])
    v=v-np.mean(v)
    n=v.size
    m=np.zeros(n); u=np.zeros(n)
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    a=softmax_from_v(v); best_sc=exact_score(a); best_v=v.copy(); best_a=a.copy()
    for t in range(1,n_steps+1):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       f,g=lse_grad_v(v,beta)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): break
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*g
       u=b2*u+(1-b2)*g*g
       mhat=m/(1-b1**t)
       uhat=u/(1-b2**t)
       v=v - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(uhat)+eps)
       v=v-np.mean(v)
       # if v range grows
       if t == 20 or np.max(np.abs(v)) > 50.0:
           v=np.clip(v,-50.0,50.0)
           v=v-np.mean(v)
       if t % verbose == 0:
           a=softmax_from_v(v); sc=exact_score(a)
           if sc < best_sc:
              best_sc=sc; best_v=v.copy(); best_a=a.copy()
              print(f"    AdamV beta={beta:.0f} t={t}: {sc:.8f}")
    a=softmax_from_v(v); sc=exact_score(a)
    if sc<best_sc: ...
    return best_sc,best_v,best_a
```
Use `gscale` not needed. But Adam uses scaled gradient if gscale=1. g ~0.0006; Adam lr 0.1. Good.

def coordinate_descent(a_in, deadline, top_k=15, max_sweeps=100):

`block_search`:
```
def block_search(a_in, deadline, rng, max_trials=20000):
   a=normalize_seq(a_in); n=len(a); best_sc=exact_score(a); best_a=a.copy()
   for t in range(max_trials):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      a_try=best_a.copy()
      min_w=max(4,n//1024)
      max_w=max(min_w+1,n//2)
      # random width log uniform
      lw=np.log(max_w/min_w)*rng.random() + np.log(min_w)
      w=int(max(min_w,min(max_w,np.exp(lw))))
      w=min(w,n//2)
      st=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
      a_try[st:st+w] *= float(rng.uniform(0.2,3.0))
      S=a_try.sum()
      if S<1e-12: continue
      a_try /= S
      sc=exact_score(a_try)
      if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a_try.copy()
          if (t//100)==1? print
   return best_sc,best_a
```
Use `rng=integers(n-w+1)` requires n-w+1 >0; w<=n//2.

def estimate_eval_time(n,beta=100):

Let's write `make_shape` carefully:
```
def make_shape(n, kind=0, rng=None):
    n=int(n)
    if n<=0: return np.ones(1)
    x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    if n==1:
        return np.ones(1)
    if kind==0:
        a=np.ones(n)
    elif kind==1:
        a=1.0+0.95*np.cos(np.pi*x/(n-1.0))
    elif kind==2:
        a=1.0+0.85*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x/(n-1.0))
    elif kind==3:
        a=np.abs(np.sin(np.pi*x/(n-1.0)))
    elif kind==4:
        if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
        a=1.0+0.4*rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif kind==5:
        c1=0.18*n; c2=0.82*n; w=0.12*n
        a=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c1)/w)**2)+np.exp(-0.5*((x-c2)/w)**2)
    elif kind==7:
        if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
        f1=rng.uniform(1.0,4.0); f2=rng.uniform(5.0,12.0)
        p1=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); p2=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
        a=1.0+0.45*np.cos(2*np.pi*f1*x/n+p1)+0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*f2*x/n+p2)
    else:
        a=np.ones(n)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: a=np.ones(n)
    else: a=a/S
    return a
```
For kind=1: cos(pi*x/(n-1)) from 1 at x=0 to -1 at x=n-1, so U-shaped with zero at center if 1-0.95=0.05. Nonnegative. Good.
kind=2: cos(2π x/(n-1)) periodic; high at ends and center? x=n-1 cos2π=1 high; x=(n-1)/2 cosπ=-1 low. U-shaped. Good.

if old==1: return np.full(new_n,1.0/new_n)
```

def adaptive_beta(M, n):
    if M is None or M <= 0: return 1000.0
    beta = 20.0 / M
    beta = max(1000.0, beta)
    beta = min(200000.0, beta)
    return float(beta)

Let's implement `active_grad_v(v)`:
```
def active_grad_v(v):
   a=softmax_from_v(v); n=len(a); c=fast_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
   if M<=0: return 0.0, np.zeros(n)
   beta=adaptive_beta(M,n)
   z=beta*(c-M)  # <=0
   # To avoid underflow if z very negative, exp safe <=1
   e=np.exp(z)
   esum=float(e.sum())
   if esum<=0: p=np.zeros_like(c); idx=argmax; p[idx]=1; else p=e/esum
   r=compute_r(a,p)
   grad=4.0*n*a*(r - M*a)
   grad -= np.mean(grad)
   return M, grad
```
Actually if z all 0 for many max, e_sum count active. Good.
This uses exact M in gradient. For subgradient, weights over exactly active? Because z=0 for c=M; others negative. If many just below within 1e-15, weights tiny. Good. Define all helpers. Let's write each carefully.

`normalize_seq`:
```
def normalize_seq(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0:
        return np.ones(1)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/max(1,a.size))
    a = a / S
    return a
```
If size 0, returns length 1; okay.

`fast_conv`:
Use direct for n <=96 maybe.

`score_from_conv` not needed; `exact_score`.

`exact_score`:
```
def exact_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fast_conv(a)
    M=float(c.max())
    if M < 0: M=0.0
    return float(2.0*a.size*M/(S*S))
```
If S huge? no.

`compute_r` as above.

`adaptive_beta` above.

`softmax_from_v` and `init_v_from_a`.

`lse_grad_v`: Need if beta<=1? use active? We'll only call beta high. But implement fallback beta=1.
```
def lse_grad_v(v,beta,gscale=1.0):
    a=softmax_from_v(v)
    n=a.size
    c=fast_conv(a)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if beta <= 0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        beta=adaptive_beta(cmax,n)
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    # z should <=0 but due roundoff maybe small positive; clip to 0?
    z=np.minimum(z,0.0)
    # max z maybe 0; no need zmax
    e=np.exp(z)
    esum=float(e.sum())
    if esum <= 0 or not np.isfinite(esum):
        p=np.zeros(c.size); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; L=cmax
    else:
        p=e/esum
        L=float(cmax + float(np.log(esum))/beta)
    r=compute_r(a,p)
    grad=4.0*n*a*(r - L*a)
    grad-=float(np.mean(grad))
    if not np.isfinite(gsum)? use term.
    f=float(2.0*n*L*gscale)
    grad=grad*gscale
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=zeros
    return f,grad
```
Clipping z to 0 ensures esum≥1, L≥cmax. Good. If due cmax not unique, z=0 for active. Good.

`active_grad_v`:
```
def active_grad_v(v):
   a=softmax_from_v(v); n=len; c=fast_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
   if M <=0: return 0, zeros
   beta=adaptive_beta(M,n)
   z=beta*(c-M); z=np.minimum(z,0)
   e=np.exp(z); esum=e.sum()
   if esum<=0: p=zeros; p[argmax]=1 else p=e/esum
   r=compute_r(a,p)
   grad=4.0*n*a*(r - M*a); grad-=mean
   if not finite: zeros
   return M,grad
```

Pseudo final:

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=min(float(budget_s),2400.0)
    if total < 60: return [1.0]*64
    deadline=t0+total-30
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        if raw is not None:
            p=np.asarray(list(raw),dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
            if p.size>=2 and p.sum()>1e-6:
                parent=normalize_seq(p)
    except Exception:
        parent=None
    if parent is not None and parent.size>=16:
        best_a=parent.copy()
        print(...)
    else:
        best_a=make_shape(6962,0)
    if best_a.size > 20000:
        best_a=resample_sequence(best_a,16384)
    best_a=normalize_seq(best_a)
    best_sc=exact_score(best_a)
    print...
    n=len(best_a)

    # simple candidates
    for kind in [1,2,3,5,7]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        cand=make_shape(n,kind,rng)
        sc=exact_score(cand)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc=sc; best_a=cand; n=len(cand); print
    cand=normalize_seq(0.5*(best_a+best_a[::-1])); sc=... update

    # coarse search
    coarse_deadline = t0 + min(90.0, (deadline-t0)*0.10)
    if time.monotonic() < coarse_deadline - 10:
       small_n = max(128, min(1024, n//4))
       starts = [resample_sequence(best_a, small_n)]
       for kind in [0,1,2,5,7]: starts.append(make_shape(small_n,kind,rng))
       for st in starts:
          if time > coarse_deadline-10: break
          v=init_v_from_a(st)
          sc,vb,ab=adam_active_search(v,50000,0.15,min(time+20,coarse_deadline-5),verbose=5000)
          if sc<best_sc: best...
          # lbfgs short
          if time<coarse_deadline-5 and HAVE_SCIPY:
             M=0; c=fast_conv(ab); M=c.max(); beta=adaptive_beta(M,small_n)
             sc0,sc1,v0,v1,a0,a1=run_lbfgs_v(vb,beta,maxiter=300,maxfun=800,gscale=max(10,min(500,0.2*small_n)))
             if sc1<best_sc: best...
          up=resample_sequence(ab,n)
          scup=exact_score(up)
          if scup<best_sc: best...
```

def run_lbfgs_v(v0,beta,maxiter=500,maxfun=2000,gscale=1.0):
    v0=np.asarray(v0,float).copy(); v0-=np.mean(v0)
    a0=softmax_from_v(v0); sc0=exact_score(a0)
    if not HAVE_SCIPY or maxfun<=0:
        return sc0, sc0, v0, v0, a0, a0
    maxiter=max(10, int(maxiter)); maxfun=max(10,int(maxfun))
    options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-6,'maxcor':50,'maxls':20}
    try:
       res=minimize(fun_v_lse, v0, args=(beta, gscale), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
       v1=np.asarray(res.x,float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(v1)): v1=v0.copy()
       v1-=np.mean(v1)
    except Exception:
       v1=v0.copy()
    a1=softmax_from_v(v1); sc1=exact_score(a1)
    if not np.isfinite(sc1): sc1=sc0; v1=v0; a1=a0
    return sc0,sc1,v0,v1,a0,a1

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function coefficients minimizing the given
self-convolution ratio.

Strategy:
- Work on the simplex (sum = 1), using a softmax/log parametrization.
- Use adaptively tempered active-set gradients: the temperature is chosen
  from the current peak convolution value so the smoothing is comparable
  to the scale of the peaks.
- Combine:
  * Adam-like log-space gradient search with active-set subgradients.
  * L-BFGS-B polishing on a smooth log-sum-exp approximation.
  * Exact coordinate descent on the most influential coordinates.
  * Multi-scale random block perturbations.
  * Length variation (resampling) and final polishing.
- The final answer is verified with the provided evaluate_sequence().
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

MAX_N = 16000


def normalize_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size))
    return a / s


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.asarray([])
    if n <= 96:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        m = 2 * n - 1
        nfft = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)[:m]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_conv(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def compute_r(a, p):
    """
    r_i = sum_k p_k a_{k-i}, with valid indices only.
    This is the gradient kernel for active-lag weights p.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = p.size
    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    req = n + L - 1
    nfft = 1 << (req - 1).bit_length()
    pr = p[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    P = np.fft.rfft(pr, nfft)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, nfft)
    start = L - n
    end = L
    r = conv[start:end][::-1]
    return np.maximum(r, 0.0)


def adaptive_beta(M, n):
    """
    Choose a smoothing temperature comparable to the peak scale.
    """
    if M is None or M <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return 1000.0
    beta = 20.0 / M
    beta = max(1000.0, beta)
    beta = min(300000.0, beta)
    return float(beta)


def softmax_from_v(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.asarray([])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return np.ones(n) / n
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    mx = float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(v - mx)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n) / n
    return e / s


def init_v_from_a(a, floor=1e-10):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.asarray([])
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n)
    else:
        a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = a / float(a.sum())
    v = np.log(a)
    return v - float(np.mean(v))


def active_grad_v(v):
    """
    Log-space gradient for an active-peak smoothed objective.
    Returns (peak value, gradient).
    """
    a = softmax_from_v(v)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.asarray([])
    c = fast_conv(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 1e-15:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n)

    beta = adaptive_beta(M, n)
    z = beta * (c - M)
    # z should be <= 0; clip away tiny roundoff positives.
    z = np.minimum(z, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    esum = float(e.sum())
    if esum <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(esum):
        p = np.zeros(c.size)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = e / esum

    r = compute_r(a, p)
    grad = 4.0 * n * a * (r - M * a)
    grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n)
    return M, grad


def lse_grad_v(v, beta, gscale=1.0):
    """
    Smooth log-sum-exp objective and gradient in log/softmax space.
    """
    a = softmax_from_v(v)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.asarray([])

    c = fast_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if beta is None or beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        beta = adaptive_beta(cmax, n)

    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    z = np.minimum(z, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    esum = float(e.sum())
    if esum <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(esum):
        p = np.zeros(c.size)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = e / esum
        L = float(cmax + float(np.log(esum)) / beta)

    r = compute_r(a, p)
    grad = 4.0 * n * a * (r - L * a)
    grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n)
        L = cmax

    f = float(2.0 * n * L * gscale)
    grad = grad * float(gscale)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e30
        grad = np.zeros(n)
    return f, grad


def fun_v_lse(v, beta, gscale=1.0):
    try:
        f, g = lse_grad_v(v, beta, gscale)
        if not np.isfinite(f):
            f = 1e30
            g = np.zeros_like(v)
        return f, g
    except Exception:
        return 1e30, np.zeros_like(v)


def run_lbfgs_v(v0, beta, maxiter=500, maxfun=2000, gscale=1.0):
    """
    L-BFGS-B on log/softmax variables.
    Returns:
      sc0, sc1, v0, v1, a0, a1
    """
    v0 = np.asarray(v0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    v0 = v0 - float(np.mean(v0))
    a0 = softmax_from_v(v0)
    sc0 = exact_score(a0)

    if not HAVE_SCIPY or maxfun <= 0:
        return sc0, sc0, v0, v0, a0, a0

    maxiter = max(20, int(maxiter))
    maxfun = max(20, int(maxfun))

    options = {
        "maxiter": maxiter,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-6,
        "maxls": 20,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun_v_lse,
            v0,
            args=(float(beta), float(gscale)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options=options,
        )
        v1 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(v1)):
            v1 = v0.copy()
        v1 = v1 - float(np.mean(v1))
    except Exception:
        v1 = v0.copy()

    a1 = softmax_from_v(v1)
    sc1 = exact_score(a1)
    if not np.isfinite(sc1):
        return sc0, sc0, v0, v0, a0, a0
    return sc0, sc1, v0, v1, a0, a1


def estimate_eval_time(n, beta=None):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY or n <= 0:
        return 1e-3
    v = np.zeros(n)
    if beta is None:
        beta = adaptive_beta(1.0 / n, n)
    try:
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        lse_grad_v(v, float(beta), 1.0)
        dt = time.monotonic() - t0
        return max(1e-5, dt)
    except Exception:
        return 1e-3


def adam_active_search(v0, n_steps, lr, deadline, verbose=5000):
    """
    Adam-like log-space search using active-set gradient.
    """
    v = np.asarray(v0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if v.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), v, np.asarray([])
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    n = v.size

    m = np.zeros(n)
    u = np.zeros(n)
    b1, b2 = 0.9, 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    a = softmax_from_v(v)
    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    best_v = v.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()

    if verbose <= 0:
        verbose = max(1, int(n_steps))

    for t in range(1, int(n_steps) + 1):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        _, g = active_grad_v(v)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
        u = b2 * u + (1.0 - b2) * g * g
        mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        uhat = u / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        step = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(uhat) + eps)

        v = v - step
        v = v - float(np.mean(v))

        if t == 20 or float(np.max(np.abs(v))) > 50.0:
            v = np.clip(v, -50.0, 50.0)
            v = v - float(np.mean(v))

        if t % verbose == 0:
            a = softmax_from_v(v)
            sc = exact_score(a)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_v = v.copy()
                best_a = a.copy()
                print(f"    AdamActive t={t}: {sc:.8f}")

    a = softmax_from_v(v)
    sc = exact_score(a)
    if sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        best_v = v.copy()
        best_a = a.copy()

    return best_sc, best_v, best_a


def coordinate_descent(a_in, deadline, top_k=12, max_sweeps=200):
    a = normalize_seq(a_in)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    ratios = np.array([0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.7, 0.85, 1.15, 1.4, 1.8, 2.5])
    top_k = max(1, min(top_k, n // 2))

    for sweep in range(int(max_sweeps)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        a = normalize_seq(a)
        c = fast_conv(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M <= 1e-15:
            break

        order_lags = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:top_k]
        vals = c[order_lags]
        weights = vals - M + 1.0
        if float(weights.sum()) <= 0:
            weights = np.ones_like(weights)
        weights = weights / float(weights.sum())

        p = np.zeros(c.size)
        p[order_lags] = weights
        r = compute_r(a, p)

        # Use M (not weighted average) for a subgradient-like direction.
        grad = 4.0 * n * a * (r - M * a)
        order_coords = np.argsort(np.abs(grad))[::-1][:top_k]

        current_sc = exact_score(a)

        for j in order_coords:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break

            old = float(a[j])
            best_val = old
            best_local_sc = current_sc
            best_local_a = a.copy()

            candidates = old * ratios
            if old < 10.0 / n:
                candidates = np.concatenate(
                    (candidates, [0.01 / n, 0.05 / n, 0.2 / n, 1.0 / n, 3.0 / n])
                )

            for val in candidates:
                val = float(val)
                if not np.isfinite(val) or val < 0.0:
                    continue
                if abs(val - old) <= 1e-15:
                    continue

                a[j] = val
                sc = exact_score(a)
                if sc < best_local_sc:
                    best_local_sc = sc
                    best_val = val
                    best_local_a = a.copy()

            a[j] = old

            if best_local_sc < current_sc - 1e-15:
                a = best_local_a.copy()
                current_sc = best_local_sc

        a = normalize_seq(a)
        sc = exact_score(a)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_sc, best_a


def block_search(a_in, deadline, rng, max_trials=50000):
    a = normalize_seq(a_in)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)

    for t in range(int(max_trials)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        if n < 8:
            a_try = best_a.copy()
            a_try *= float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0))
        else:
            a_try = best_a.copy()
            min_w = max(4, n // 2048)
            max_w = max(min_w + 1, n // 2)
            if max_w > min_w:
                log_ratio = rng.uniform(0.0, float(np.log2(max_w / max(min_w, 1))))
                w = int(min_w * (2.0 ** log_ratio))
            else:
                w = min_w
            w = int(min(max_w, max(min_w, w)))
            w = min(w, n // 2)
            if w <= 0:
                continue
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
            factor = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
            a_try[start:start + w] *= factor

        s = float(a_try.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            continue
        a_try = a_try / s
        sc = exact_score(a_try)

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            if (t + 1) % 1000 == 0:
                print(f"    BlockSearch t={t+1}: {sc:.8f}")

    return best_sc, best_a


def resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old_n = a.size
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return normalize_seq(a)
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n) / new_n
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.ones(new_n) / new_n

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    a_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    a_new = np.clip(a_new, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return normalize_seq(a_new)


def make_shape(n, kind=0, rng=None):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1)

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if kind == 0:
        a = np.ones(n)
    elif kind == 1:
        a = 1.0 + 0.95 * np.cos(np.pi * x / (n - 1.0))
    elif kind == 2:
        a = 1.0 + 0.85 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x / (n - 1.0))
    elif kind == 3:
        a = np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * x / (n - 1.0)))
    elif kind == 4:
        if rng is None:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
        a = 1.0 + 0.4 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif kind == 5:
        c1 = 0.18 * n
        c2 = 0.82 * n
        w = 0.12 * n
        a = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c1) / w) ** 2) + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c2) / w) ** 2)
    elif kind == 7:
        if rng is None:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(123)
        f1 = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 4.0))
        f2 = float(rng.uniform(5.0, 12.0))
        p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a = (
            1.0
            + 0.45 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x / n + p1)
            + 0.30 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x / n + p2)
        )
    else:
        a = np.ones(n)

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return normalize_seq(a)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = min(float(budget_s), 2400.0)
    if total_budget < 60.0:
        return [1.0] * 64

    deadline = t0 + total_budget - 30.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load previous global candidate, if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        if raw is not None:
            p = np.asarray(list(raw), dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 2 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-6:
                parent = normalize_seq(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 16:
        best_a = parent.copy()
        print(f"Loaded parent: n={best_a.size}")
    else:
        best_a = make_shape(6962, 0)
        print("No usable parent; starting with uniform n=6962")

    if best_a.size > MAX_N:
        best_a = resample_sequence(best_a, min(MAX_N, max(2048, best_a.size)))

    best_a = normalize_seq(best_a)
    best_sc = exact_score(best_a)
    n = int(best_a.size)
    print(f"Initial: n={n}, score={best_sc:.8f}")

    # Quick deterministic candidates at the same length.
    for kind in [1, 2, 3, 5, 7]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        cand = make_shape(n, kind, rng)
        sc = exact_score(cand)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = cand.copy()
            n = int(best_a.size)
            print(f"  shape kind={kind}: {sc:.8f}")

    cand = normalize_seq(0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1]))
    sc = exact_score(cand)
    if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
        best_sc = sc
        best_a = cand.copy()
        n = int(best_a.size)
        print(f"  symmetrized: {sc:.8f}")

    # Coarse multi-start at smaller length.
    coarse_deadline = t0 + min(90.0, (deadline - t0) * 0.10)
    if time.monotonic() < coarse_deadline - 15.0:
        small_n = max(128, min(1024, max(128, n // 4)))
        starts = [resample_sequence(best_a, small_n)]
        for kind in [0, 1, 2, 5]:
            starts.append(make_shape(small_n, kind, rng))

        for idx, st in enumerate(starts):
            if time.monotonic() > coarse_deadline - 10.0:
                break
            v = init_v_from_a(st)
            per_trial = max(5.0, (coarse_deadline - time.monotonic()) / max(1, len(starts) - idx))
            end = min(coarse_deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + min(15.0, per_trial * 0.7))
            sc, vb, ab = adam_active_search(v, 50000, 0.12, end, verbose=2000)

            if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = ab.copy()
                n = int(best_a.size)
                print(f"  coarse start #{idx}: {sc:.8f}")

            if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < coarse_deadline - 5.0:
                c = fast_conv(ab)
                M = float(c.max())
                beta = adaptive_beta(M, small_n)
                gscale = max(10.0, min(500.0, 0.2 * small_n))
                sc0, sc1, v0, v1, a0, a1 = run_lbfgs_v(
                    v if sc <= exact_score(softmax_from_v(v)) else vb,
                    beta,
                    maxiter=300,
                    maxfun=800,
                    gscale=gscale,
                )
                if sc1 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = sc1
                    best_a = a1.copy()
                    n = int(best_a.size)
                    print(f"  coarse LBFGS #{idx}: {sc1:.8f}")
                up = resample_sequence(a1, n)
                scup = exact_score(up)
                if scup < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = scup
                    best_a = up.copy()
                    n = int(best_a.size)
                    print(f"  coarse upsample #{idx}: {scup:.8f}")

    # Main log-space active-set Adam phases.
    main_start = time.monotonic()
    main_deadline = min(deadline, main_start + max(150.0, (deadline - main_start) * 0.30))
    v = init_v_from_a(best_a)
    main_duration = max(1.0, main_deadline - main_start)
    lr_list = [0.15, 0.10, 0.07, 0.05]

    for i, lr in enumerate(lr_list):
        seg_deadline = main_start + ((i + 1) / len(lr_list)) * main_duration
        if time.monotonic() > min(seg_deadline, deadline):
            break
        end = min(seg_deadline, deadline)
        sc, vb, ab = adam_active_search(v, 200000, lr, end, verbose=5000)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = ab.copy()
            n = int(best_a.size)
            v = vb.copy()
            print(f"  AdamActive phase {i}: {sc:.8f}")
        elif sc < best_sc * 1.001:
            v = vb.copy()
        else:
            v = init_v_from_a(best_a)

    # L-BFGS polishing with adaptive beta.
    if HAVE_SCIPY:
        lb_start = time.monotonic()
        lb_deadline = min(deadline, lb_start + max(300.0, (deadline - lb_start) * 0.45))
        v = init_v_from_a(best_a)
        n = int(best_a.size)
        Mbest = best_sc / (2.0 * n) if n > 0 else 1.0 / n
        base_beta = adaptive_beta(Mbest, n)
        eval_time = estimate_eval_time(n, base_beta)
        gscale = max(10.0, min(1000.0, 0.2 * n))

        for factor in [0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0]:
            if time.monotonic() > lb_deadline - 10.0:
                break
            beta = max(1000.0, min(300000.0, base_beta * factor))
            target = min(70.0, max(8.0, (lb_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.18))
            maxfun = int(min(5000, max(300, (target / max(eval_time, 1e-4)) * 0.8)))
            maxiter = int(max(300, maxfun // 2))

            sc0, sc1, v0, v1, a0, a1 = run_lbfgs_v(
                v, beta, maxiter=maxiter, maxfun=maxfun, gscale=gscale
            )
            if sc1 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc1
                best_a = a1.copy()
                n = int(best_a.size)
                v = v1.copy()
                Mbest = best_sc / (2.0 * n) if n > 0 else 1.0 / n
                base_beta = adaptive_beta(Mbest, n)
                eval_time = estimate_eval_time(n, base_beta)
                gscale = max(10.0, min(1000.0, 0.2 * n))
                print(f"  L-BFGS beta={beta:.0f}: {sc1:.8f}")
            elif sc1 < best_sc * 1.002:
                v = v1.copy()
            else:
                v = init_v_from_a(best_a)

    # Exact coordinate descent.
    cd_start = time.monotonic()
    cd_deadline = min(deadline, cd_start + max(60.0, (deadline - cd_start) * 0.15))
    sc, a = coordinate_descent(best_a, cd_deadline, top_k=12, max_sweeps=250)
    if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
        best_sc = sc
        best_a = a.copy()
        n = int(best_a.size)
        print(f"  Coordinate descent: {sc:.8f}")

    # Block random perturbations.
    bs_start = time.monotonic()
    bs_deadline = min(deadline, bs_start + max(30.0, (deadline - bs_start) * 0.10))
    sc, a = block_search(best_a, bs_deadline, rng, max_trials=50000)
    if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
        best_sc = sc
        best_a = a.copy()
        n = int(best_a.size)
        print(f"  Block search: {sc:.8f}")

    # Length variation / resampling.
    len_deadline = deadline - 25.0
    base_n = int(best_a.size)
    if base_n >= 256:
        for mult in [1.25, 1.5, 0.75, 2.0]:
            if time.monotonic() > len_deadline - 30.0:
                break
            new_n = int(base_n * mult)
            if new_n < 512 or new_n > MAX_N:
                continue
            if abs(new_n - base_n) < 0.05 * base_n:
                continue

            start = resample_sequence(best_a, new_n)
            sc_start = exact_score(start)

            if time.monotonic() < len_deadline - 25.0:
                v = init_v_from_a(start)
                end = min(len_deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + 20.0)
                sc, vb, ab = adam_active_search(v, 100000, 0.05, end, verbose=2500)
                if sc < sc_start - 1e-12:
                    sc_start = sc
                    start = ab.copy()

            if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < len_deadline - 5.0:
                c = fast_conv(start)
                M = float(c.max())
                beta = adaptive_beta(M, new_n)
                eval_time = estimate_eval_time(new_n, beta)
                gscale = max(10.0, min(1000.0, 0.2 * new_n))
                maxfun = int(min(2500, max(300, (25.0 / max(eval_time, 1e-4)) * 0.6)))
                maxiter = int(max(200, maxfun // 2))
                v = init_v_from_a(start)
                sc0, sc1, v0, v1, a0, a1 = run_lbfgs_v(
                    v, beta, maxiter=maxiter, maxfun=maxfun, gscale=gscale
                )
                if sc1 < sc_start - 1e-12:
                    sc_start = sc1
                    start = a1.copy()

            if sc_start < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sc_start
                best_a = start.copy()
                n = int(best_a.size)
                print(f"  length n={n} (mult {mult}): {best_sc:.8f}")

    # Final short polish.
    final_deadline = deadline - 5.0
    n = int(best_a.size)
    if HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < final_deadline and n > 0:
        v = init_v_from_a(best_a)
        Mbest = best_sc / (2.0 * n) if best_sc > 0 else 1.0 / n
        beta = adaptive_beta(Mbest, n)
        eval_time = estimate_eval_time(n, beta)
        gscale = max(10.0, min(1000.0, 0.2 * n))
        maxfun = int(min(1500, max(200, (18.0 / max(eval_time, 1e-4)) * 0.7)))
        maxiter = int(max(200, maxfun // 2))
        sc0, sc1, v0, v1, a0, a1 = run_lbfgs_v(
            v, beta, maxiter=maxiter, maxfun=maxfun, gscale=gscale
        )
        if sc1 < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc1
            best_a = a1.copy()
            n = int(best_a.size)
            print(f"  Final L-BFGS: {sc1:.8f}")

    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline:
        sc, a = coordinate_descent(best_a, final_deadline, top_k=8, max_sweeps=50)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            n = int(best_a.size)
            print(f"  Final coordinate descent: {sc:.8f}")

    # Final sanitation and validation against the actual evaluator.
    best_a = normalize_seq(best_a)
    candidates = [best_a]

    if parent is not None and parent.size <= MAX_N and parent.size != int(best_a.size):
        candidates.append(parent)

    candidates.append(np.ones(int(best_a.size), dtype=np.float64) / int(best_a.size))

    best_list = None
    best_eval = float("inf")

    for cand in candidates:
        arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(arr.sum()) < 0.01:
            arr = np.full(64, 0.5)
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]

        val = None
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except NameError:
            val = float(exact_score(arr))
        except Exception:
            val = float("inf")

        print(f"  final candidate len={len(lst)} evaluate={val:.8f}")
        if val < best_eval:
            best_eval = val
            best_list = lst

    if best_list is None or len(best_list) == 0:
        best_list = [1.0] * 64

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print(f"FINAL: internal={best_sc:.8f}, evaluator={best_eval:.8f}, length={len(best_list)}, time={elapsed:.1f}s")
    return best_list
```
